Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the search object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 845. The moduli space has dimension at most 364 with 5 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on search structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 845. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 364. QED.
